## Mandatory successor synchronization contract

- **At the start of every successor BlueLite session:** fetch this status from `https://github.com/johnny-belgrade/windows/blob/main/BlueLite_Current_Status.md`, plus live Master Strategy; do not rely on stale handoff snapshots.
- **Mandatory internal BlueLite Library check:** During each successor-session startup, search the operator's internal ChatGPT Library for relevant original scripts, logs, audits, `Build_info`, UUP inputs, forensic comparisons and successor handoffs; read material matches before proposing a new technical action. Repeat a target-specific Library lookup whenever researching a component/dependency or revising a production rule. Library documents are evidence candidates, not proof of a current WIM state, operator execution, runtime certification or mutation authorization. If a relevant file is not accessible, report the evidence gap without claiming it was inspected (Master v2.5 §4.1 and §30.1).
- **After every operator-confirmed significant milestone:** update this same GitHub file, first rereading the latest blob SHA, merging verified progress, and independently refetching the post-commit content. A delivered script is not proof of execution.
- **Operator communication requirement (2026-10-13):** After **every** BlueLite assistant-delivered step, command, or confirmation, synchronize the corresponding verified state or explicitly marked unexecuted candidate to this GitHub Current Status and show the operator `[GitHub Current Status](https://github.com/johnny-belgrade/windows/blob/main/BlueLite_Current_Status.md) ažuriran — commit <actual SHA>` only after a successful update and independent refetch. Never invent commits or mark proposed scripts executed. If an update fails, explicitly report `GITHUB STATUS NOT SYNCED` and the failure; do not claim completion.
- **At productive session close / handoff:** ensure the latest milestone, certification tier, blockers, and one next operator-approved action are recorded here. No implied permission for UUP download or offline mutation.
- **If GitHub write fails:** report `GITHUB STATUS NOT SYNCED`; keep a local candidate, never claim a remote update. No PAT/token disclosure in chat or repository.
- Standing instruction source: Master Strategy v2.5.2, **§30.1**, and historical Handoff_4, **§0A.5**.

## Historical offline/component intelligence and external-image lessons — HANDOFF_10

- **Earlier BlueLite 26200.9278 lineage — historically operator-reported offline changes (NOT a portable removal preset):** People/Persona ~17 unique/13–14 MB; Cortana/Search *web-only* cache 211 unique/33.76 MB; CoreAI 193 exclusive/26.82 MB with **17 shared/2.07 MB retained and accidental shared ACL ownership restored to TrustedInstaller**; AIX/Recall ~44.36 MB with shared DLLs retained; Sense/Endpoint ~173 unique/413.09 MB physical-carve scenario; Edge DevTools ~401 unique/10.71 MB. Source detail and evidence locator: HANDOFF_10 §§4.2–4.6, Appendix B. **These measurements have different denominators (unique bytes vs names/hardlinks) and must not be added to claim a final ISO size or replayed blindly on 9445/9550.**
- **Client.CBS owner/dependency floor:** DiscoveryHub AppX application/protocol and DragTray/FeatureDiscoverability extensions are embedded in broad `userexperience-aot` + `userexperience-desktop` and shared `OSClient.NET.dll`/`OSClient.UWP.dll`; signed/blockmapped `AppxManifest.xml` and WinSxS hardlinks constrain physical edits. DiscoveryHub+Widget assets: **33 files/234,529 B** and DragTray DLL **588,800 B** were forensically inspected; shared owner removal for those tiny surfaces offers no comparable unique-store saving. Shell Update DiscoveryHub hash `7eb2979c...` shared by **17 components**. **DCM/PA30** manifests were decoded using offline `wcp.dll` servicing dictionary/resource and `UpdateCompression.dll`; retain this advanced read-only method, not the false assumption that raw compressed manifest is XML.
- **WU/Store/Defender dependencies:** preserve reversible Windows Update UI `Resume` while pausing to 31-Dec-2100, distinguish `WaaSMedicSvc` disable from removal of `wuauserv/UsoSvc/BITS/DoSvc` core; Store AppX removal does **not** justify removing OS `LicenseManager`/`StartTileData.dll` activation consumers; keep Defender AV and Sense/Endpoint distinct, and preserve Defender ON branching floor. `WSearch` indexing vs Search UI are separate. AppX provisioning, user SID/StateRepository, signed manifest and first-logon registration require distinct gates (HANDOFF_10 §§4–6).
- **Risky but valuable offline AMBER avenues, never auto-run:** physical WinSxS payload carve with retained MUM/CAT/COMPONENTS, observed pre/post CBS Owners/CurrentState delta, target-scoped ACL/`SeTakeOwnershipPrivilege`/native registry key deletion, symlink/hardlink-aware rollback, DCM manifest decode, selective AppX runtime surface neutralization, superseded FoD cleanup. Historical failures include `0x800F0825` Sense capability removal, `0x80070005` package/ACL denial, partial mutation before registry failure, orphan invalid DISM mounts and loaded hive handles. **Always require exact build/owner graph, external immutable backup, scope/path and foreign-consumer checks, health/rollback, and independent operator authorization.**
- **FBConan reference image — do not imitate the wrong metric:** multi-index Defender OFF/ON **solid WIM ~2.845 GB**, each image logical ~17.17 GB; post-install `SetupComplete.cmd` used `compact /c /s /i /a /exe:XPRESS8K` despite `CompactOS` OFF; captured Admin RID 1000 and earlier AppReadiness changed measured first-logon cost. WinSxS Backup/Migration absent but `Catalogs/FileMaps/Manifests` retained, plus native CBS `COMPONENTS` f!/c! lifecycle. Original reference had a **repairable** health finding; not a release health model. Compare *actual unique physical bytes, transport compression, new-user first boot, and servicing* separately (HANDOFF_10 §5).
- **Carry forward to NTLite/VM/ISO only after offline intelligence:** keep optional MSIX/REG/Desktop scripts, drivers/network/VM floor, Defender ON/OFF, Store-free base; in VirtualBox test fresh profile, new-user AppX, Wi-Fi/network, Shell/Settings, licensing, WinUpdate `Pause/Resume`, and measured first boot. Optional XPRESS8K file compression, solid multi-index WIM and SetupComplete cleanup are **A/B size/performance experiments**, not proven physical debloat. NTLite Business remains blocked until GREEN/AMBER residual intelligence exhausted and post-state certified (HANDOFF_10 §§11–12).
